###############################################################################
# Bloodlines
# Namespace: bloodline (was HF_bloodline_events and
#   HF_bloodline_acquisition_events; bloodline.N = old HF.24N, HF.99/100/110/120
#   are bloodline.270-273, acquisition HF.N is bloodline.(N+200))
# Faction: Family & bloodlines
# Serves: all; the special bloodlines are for Chaos Dwarfs of Zharr-Naggrund, Border Princes sea-queens, Amazons, pious Sigmarite lords and human unifiers of the Border Princes
#
# Earned bloodlines: forge one through the ambition (murder, war, peace and
# building paths and their hybrids), through an event chain (benevolent,
# family, theologian, technology, zealot, ruthless), or win one of the
# special bloodlines below. Owning a bloodline then fires its own flavour:
# murder children and banquets, knights and bards at court, drills, gifts,
# crackdowns and dynastic meddling.
#
# Founder legacy (warrior, sympathy, personality)  bloodline.270 - 273
# Hashut bull: Bull-Tyrant of Zharr-Naggrund       bloodline.0 - 6
# Teuta: Border Princes sea-queen, corsair ships   bloodline.10 - 16
# Forge Bloodline ambition: counters               bloodline.17 - 24
# Ambition paths (murder, war, peace, building)    bloodline.25 - 51, 231
# Event-chain bloodlines                           bloodline.52 - 73
# Murder bloodline flavour (child, banquet,        bloodline.100 - 128
#   deflection, plots)
# Knight, munificent, crackdown flavour            bloodline.129 - 151
# Family bloodline: dynastic meddling              bloodline.152 - 173
# The First Queen reborn (Amazons): the Elders'    bloodline.220 - 229
#   trials, the Sisters of the First Queen guard
# Heir who has not lived up to the founder         bloodline.230
# Dragon bloodline                                 bloodline.232 - 235
# Technology bloodline: scholars at court          bloodline.236 - 240
# Sword of the Temple (Sigmar vs a heretic         bloodline.250 - 253
#   Emperor)
# Greenskin exterminator, greenthumb, guardian,    bloodline.254 - 258
#   corruptor
# The Iron Prince: unite the Border Princes        bloodline.260 - 262
#   (humans only, once a game)
# Child of Destiny                                 bloodline.399 - 506
#
# Saved: event targets defeated_emperor, scoped_pope (the Grand Theogonist),
#   pirate_bloodline_target, target_bloodline_founder, child_of_destiny;
#   global flags flag_phalaris_bloodline, flag_teuta_bloodline,
#   flag_ashoka_blood_restored, flag_matilde_bloodline, flag_iron_prince_bloodline (each special
#   bloodline once per game); character flag bloodline_skip_after_effects.
# Rewards: Teuta's corsair ships (13-16); the Sisters of the First Queen,
#   150 light infantry and 100 archers (228, earmark
#   bloodline_first_queen_sisters); the Iron Prince's free company (261, 10%:
#   100 light cavalry, 150 light infantry, 50 archers, earmark
#   bloodline_iron_prince_company).
# Support: common/bloodlines/02_earned_bloodlines.txt,
#   decisions/HF_bloodline_decisions.txt, rip_torture_decisions.txt,
#   imprisoned_events.txt (brazen bull), common/objectives/00_HF_ambitions.txt.
# Loc: localisation/00_WHG_bloodline_events.csv
###############################################################################

namespace = bloodline

character_event = { # Great warrior character chance
	id = bloodline.270
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_playable = yes
	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no
	min_age = 16

	trigger = {
		AND = {
			OR = {
				any_owned_bloodline = {
					has_bloodline_flag = bloodline_heroes
					bloodline_is_active_for = PREV
					founder = { NOT = { character = ROOT } religion = ROOT }
				}
				AND = {
					capital_scope = { has_wonder_upgrade = upgrade_shrine_grimnir }
					any_realm_wonder_upgrade = {
						has_wonder_upgrade = upgrade_shrine_grimnir
						is_active = yes
					}
				}
			}
			NOT = {
				any_courtier_or_vassal = {
					count = 4
					OR = {
						has_character_flag = bloodline_great_hero
						has_character_flag = slayer_summon
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			80 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 NOT = { higher_tier_than = COUNT } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 NOT = { higher_tier_than = DUKE } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.75
					any_courtier_or_vassal = { count = 2 has_character_flag = bloodline_great_hero }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.75
					any_courtier_or_vassal = { count = 3 has_character_flag = bloodline_great_hero }
				}
				character_event = { id = bloodline.271 }
			}
			20 = { modifier = { factor = 3 ai = yes } }
		}
	}
}

character_event = { # Great warrior character arrives
	id = bloodline.271
	desc = bloodline271_DESC
	picture = GFX_evt_battle
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_playable = yes
	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no
	min_age = 16

	immediate = {
		random_owned_bloodline = {
			limit = {
				has_bloodline_flag = bloodline_heroes
				founder = { NOT = { character = ROOT } religion = ROOT }
			}
			founder = { save_event_target_as = target_bloodline_founder }
		}
		spawn_fantastic_commander_effect = yes
		event_target:invited_character = {
			set_character_flag = bloodline_great_hero
			set_character_flag = hero_knight
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					capital_scope = { has_wonder_upgrade = upgrade_shrine_grimnir }
					any_realm_wonder_upgrade = {
						has_wonder_upgrade = upgrade_shrine_grimnir
						is_active = yes
					}
				}
			}
			event_target:invited_character = {
				add_trait = dwarf_slayer
				set_character_flag = slayer_summon
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = MNM.70013.o

		trigger = {
			any_realm_character = {
				NOT = { higher_tier_than = BARON }
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_commander
				NOT = { martial = 12 }
			}
			OR = {
				AND = {
					tier = COUNT
					any_realm_character = {
						count = 2
						liege = { character = ROOT }
						has_minor_title = title_commander
					}
				}
				AND = {
					tier = DUKE
					any_realm_character = {
						count = 4
						liege = { character = ROOT }
						has_minor_title = title_commander
					}
				}
				AND = {
					tier = KING
					is_nomadic = no
					any_realm_character = {
						count = 6
						liege = { character = ROOT }
						has_minor_title = title_commander
					}
				}
				AND = {
					tier = EMPEROR
					is_nomadic = no
					any_realm_character = {
						count = 8
						liege = { character = ROOT }
						has_minor_title = title_commander
					}
				}
				AND = {
					tier = KING
					is_nomadic = yes
					any_realm_character = {
						count = 2
						liege = { character = ROOT }
						has_minor_title = title_commander
					}
				}
				AND = {
					tier = EMPEROR
					is_nomadic = yes
					any_realm_character = {
						count = 4
						liege = { character = ROOT }
						has_minor_title = title_commander
					}
				}
			}
		}

		random_realm_character = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { higher_tier_than = BARON }
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_commander
				NOT = { martial = 12 }
			}
			remove_title = title_commander
			opinion = { modifier = insulted who = ROOT years = 2 }
		}

		event_target:invited_character = {
			give_minor_title = title_commander
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_loyal_servant who = ROOT years = 100 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_NE_1

		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { ROOT = { has_law = succ_byzantine_elective } } #Either not Imperial...
				NOT = { #...Or there are no valid vassal candidates that can be commanders.
					any_vassal = {
						higher_real_tier_than = BARON
						NOT = { primary_title = { temporary = yes } }
						OR = {
							AND = {
								OR = {
									male_can_hold_minor_title_trigger = yes
									female_can_hold_minor_commander_title_trigger = yes
								}
								is_adult = yes
							}
							has_character_flag = special_marshal
						}

						prisoner = no
						NOT = { trait = incapable }
						NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
						NOT = { has_character_flag = guru }
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = refusing_to_lead }
						NOR = {
							has_job_title = job_chancellor
							has_job_title = job_marshal
							has_job_title = job_treasurer
							has_job_title = job_spymaster
							has_job_title = job_spiritual
							has_minor_title = title_commander
						}
					}
				}
			}
			event_target:invited_character = { has_character_flag = invited_soldier }
			OR = {
				AND = {
					tier = COUNT
					NOT = {
						any_realm_character = {
							count = 2
							liege = { character = ROOT }
							has_minor_title = title_commander
						}
					}
				}
				AND = {
					tier = DUKE
					NOT = {
						any_realm_character = {
							count = 4
							liege = { character = ROOT }
							has_minor_title = title_commander
						}
					}
				}
				AND = {
					tier = KING
					is_nomadic = no
					NOT = {
						any_realm_character = {
							count = 6
							liege = { character = ROOT }
							has_minor_title = title_commander
						}
					}
				}
				AND = {
					tier = EMPEROR
					is_nomadic = no
					NOT = {
						any_realm_character = {
							count = 8
							liege = { character = ROOT }
							has_minor_title = title_commander
						}
					}
				}
				AND = {
					tier = KING
					is_nomadic = yes
					NOT = {
						any_realm_character = {
							count = 2
							liege = { character = ROOT }
							has_minor_title = title_commander
						}
					}
				}
				AND = {
					tier = EMPEROR
					is_nomadic = yes
					NOT = {
						any_realm_character = {
							count = 4
							liege = { character = ROOT }
							has_minor_title = title_commander
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		event_target:invited_character = {
			give_minor_title = title_commander
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_loyal_servant who = ROOT years = 100 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = FANTASTIC
		event_target:invited_character = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_loyal_servant who = ROOT years = 100 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_bloodline271

		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:invited_character = {
				if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes }
				else = { death = { death_reason = death_missing } }
			}
		}
	}
}

character_event = { # Sympathy for founder group
	id = bloodline.272
	desc = bloodline272_DESC
	picture = GFX_evt_council
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_playable = yes
	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no
	min_age = 16

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_flag = no_sympathy_bloodline }
		any_owned_bloodline = {
			bloodline_is_active_for = PREV
			founder = {
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				is_alive = no
				OR = {
					AND = {
						religion_group = old_world_group
						ROOT = {
							NOR = {
								religion_group = old_world_group
								trait = sympathy_old_world_group
							}
						}
					}
					AND = {
						religion_group = ormazd_gods_group
						ROOT = {
							NOR = {
								religion_group = ormazd_gods_group
								trait = sympathy_ormazd_gods_group
							}
						}
					}
					AND = {
						religion_group = cadai_group
						ROOT = {
							NOR = { religion_group = cadai_group trait = sympathy_cadai_group }
						}
					}
					AND = {
						religion_group = dwarf_gods_group
						ROOT = {
							NOR = {
								religion_group = dwarf_gods_group
								trait = sympathy_dwarf_gods_group
							}
						}
					}
					AND = {
						religion_group = oriental_gods_group
						ROOT = {
							NOR = {
								religion_group = oriental_gods_group
								trait = sympathy_oriental_gods_group
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_owned_bloodline = {
			limit = {
				founder = {
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					is_alive = no
					OR = {
						AND = {
							religion_group = old_world_group
							ROOT = {
								NOR = {
									religion_group = old_world_group
									trait = sympathy_old_world_group
								}
							}
						}
						AND = {
							religion_group = ormazd_gods_group
							ROOT = {
								NOR = {
									religion_group = ormazd_gods_group
									trait = sympathy_ormazd_gods_group
								}
							}
						}
						AND = {
							religion_group = cadai_group
							ROOT = {
								NOR = { religion_group = cadai_group trait = sympathy_cadai_group }
							}
						}
						AND = {
							religion_group = dwarf_gods_group
							ROOT = {
								NOR = {
									religion_group = jewish_group
									trait = sympathy_dwarf_gods_group
								}
							}
						}
						AND = {
							religion_group = oriental_gods_group
							ROOT = {
								NOR = {
									religion_group = oriental_gods_group
									trait = sympathy_oriental_gods_group
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			founder = { save_event_target_as = target_bloodline_founder }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_bloodline272
		tooltip_info_custom = lunatic

		trigger = {
			OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
			NOT = { trait = zealous }
		}

		piety = -250
		religion = event_target:target_bloodline_founder
		add_trait = zealous
		set_character_flag = ai_flag_refuse_conversion

		ai_chance = { factor = 5 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_bloodline272
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:target_bloodline_founder = { religion_group = old_world_group } }
			add_trait = sympathy_old_world_group
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_bloodline_founder = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group }
			}
			add_trait = sympathy_ormazd_gods_group
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_bloodline_founder = { religion_group = dwarf_gods_group }
			}
			add_trait = sympathy_dwarf_gods_group
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { event_target:target_bloodline_founder = { religion_group = cadai_group } }
			add_trait = sympathy_cadai_group
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_bloodline_founder = { religion_group = oriental_gods_group }
			}
			add_trait = sympathy_oriental_gods_group
		}

		ai_chance = { factor = 45 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_bloodline272
		set_character_flag = no_sympathy_bloodline
		piety = 5

		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
}

character_event = { # Chance of gaining a personality trait of Founder
	id = bloodline.273
	desc = bloodline273_DESC
	picture = GFX_evt_family
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_playable = yes
	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no
	min_age = 16

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_flag = had_bloodline_personality_event }
		any_owned_bloodline = {
			bloodline_is_active_for = PREV
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_bloodline_flag = saintly_bloodline }
				ROOT = { religion_group = old_world_group }
			}
			founder = {
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				is_alive = no
				ROOT = { can_copy_personality_trait_from = PREV }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = had_bloodline_personality_event
		random_owned_bloodline = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_bloodline_flag = saintly_bloodline }
					ROOT = { religion_group = old_world_group }
				}
				founder = {
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					is_alive = no
					ROOT = { can_copy_personality_trait_from = PREV }
				}
			}
			founder = { save_event_target_as = target_bloodline_founder }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_bloodline273
		tooltip_info_custom = lunatic
		trigger = {
			OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
			NOT = { trait = cannibal_trait }
			event_target:target_bloodline_founder = { trait = cannibal_trait }
		}
		add_trait = cannibal_trait
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_bloodline273
		tooltip_info = possessed
		trigger = {
			trait = possessed
			NOR = { trait = cannibal_trait trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
			event_target:target_bloodline_founder = { trait = cannibal_trait }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_bloodline273

		copy_random_personality_trait = event_target:target_bloodline_founder
		add_character_modifier = { name = emulating_legend years = 5 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_bloodline273
	}
}

# Hashut bull bloodline: a Chaos Dwarf tyrant of Zharr-Naggrund who roasts a prisoner in the brazen bull (torture decision, impaler event or the bull decision).
narrative_event = {
	id = bloodline.0
	title = EVTTITLEbloodline0
	desc = EVTDESCbloodline0
	picture = GFX_evt_beheading
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_intrigue
	sound = bloodline_added
	show_from_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Holy Fury"
		NOT = { has_global_flag = flag_phalaris_bloodline }
		NOT = { has_dynasty_flag = flag_phalaris_event }
		is_female = no
	}

	immediate = {
		set_dynasty_flag = flag_phalaris_event
	} #If Character says no first time, event never fires again for his dynasty.

	option = { #Gain Phalaris bloodline.
		name = EVTOPTAbloodline0
		custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_restore_brazen_bull }
		if = { limit = { has_nickname = no } give_nickname = nick_the_tyrant }
		if = {
			limit = { is_female = no }
			create_bloodline = { type = phalaris_male }
			new_bloodline = { set_description = "phalaris_male_desc" }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = brazen_bull }
				remove_character_modifier = brazen_bull #Negative opinion is now part of the bloodline itself.
			}
			any_player = {
				limit = { is_within_diplo_range = ROOT NOT = { character = ROOT } }
				narrative_event = { id = bloodline.6 }
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = flag_phalaris_bloodline
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 90
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Nothing.
		name = EVTOPTBbloodline0
		tiered_prestige_negative_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = brazen_bull }
			remove_character_modifier = brazen_bull
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = charitable }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = humble }
		}
	}
}

# Killed by brazen bull - First time.
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.1
	desc = EVTDESCbloodline1
	picture = GFX_evt_beheading
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAbloodline1
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = bloodline.2 } } }
		if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes }
		else = { death = { death_reason = death_execution_brazen_bull killer = FROM } }
	}
}

# Host notified of death by brazen bull - First time.
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.2
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_modifier = brazen_bull } text = EVTDESCAbloodline2 }
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = brazen_bull } }
		text = EVTDESCBbloodline2
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_beheading
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAbloodline2
		hidden_tooltip = {
			narrative_event = { id = bloodline.0 days = 30 random = 80 } #Apply bloodline after rumors spread.
		}
		tiered_piety_reward_effect = yes # The bull is Hashut's own rite.
	}
}

# Notice to other players: a new Tyrant of Zharr-Naggrund.
narrative_event = {
	id = bloodline.6

	desc = EVTDESCbloodline6
	picture = GFX_evt_beheading
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_intrigue

	}

# Teuta's bloodline: a Border Princes sea-queen beats Luccini, Aegos, Tilea or the Empire (on_war_ended_victory).
# From = Attacker, Root = Defender
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.10
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Holy Fury"
		NOT = { has_global_flag = flag_teuta_bloodline } #Just once.
		OR = {
			AND = { #Either won as defensive...
				ROOT = {
					is_woman_trigger = yes
					is_alternate_start = no
					independent = yes
					lower_tier_than = KING
					NOT = { realm_size = 80 }
					NOR = { culture = myken culture = lucian }
					OR = {
						culture_group = sartosan_group
						culture = marcheur
						culture = comarcano
						culture = asur_colonial
						culture = oltremari
						culture = neueslander
					}
					capital_scope = { region = world_old_world_border_princes }
				}
				FROM = {
					NOR = {
						culture_group = sartosan_group
						culture = marcheur
						culture = comarcano
						culture = asur_colonial
						culture = oltremari
						culture = neueslander
					}
					OR = {
						has_landed_title = k_luccini
						has_landed_title = k_aegos
						has_landed_title = e_tilea
						has_landed_title = e_the_empire
					}
				}
			}
			AND = { #...Or as attacker.
				FROM = {
					is_woman_trigger = yes
					is_alternate_start = no
					independent = yes
					lower_tier_than = KING
					NOT = { realm_size = 80 }
					NOR = { culture = myken culture = lucian }
					OR = {
						culture_group = sartosan_group
						culture = marcheur
						culture = comarcano
						culture = asur_colonial
						culture = oltremari
						culture = neueslander
					}
					capital_scope = { region = world_old_world_border_princes }
				}
				ROOT = {
					NOR = {
						culture_group = sartosan_group
						culture = marcheur
						culture = comarcano
						culture = asur_colonial
						culture = oltremari
						culture = neueslander
					}
					OR = {
						has_landed_title = k_luccini
						has_landed_title = k_aegos
						has_landed_title = e_tilea
						has_landed_title = e_the_empire
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		sound_effect = bloodline_added
		set_global_flag = flag_teuta_bloodline
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					is_woman_trigger = yes
					is_alternate_start = no
					independent = yes
					lower_tier_than = KING
					NOT = { realm_size = 80 }
					NOR = { culture = myken culture = lucian }
					OR = {
						culture_group = sartosan_group
						culture = marcheur
						culture = comarcano
						culture = asur_colonial
						culture = oltremari
						culture = neueslander
					}
					capital_scope = { region = world_old_world_border_princes }
				}
			}
			FROM = { save_event_target_as = defeated_emperor }
			ROOT = {
				set_character_flag = flag_defensive_teuta
				narrative_event = { id = bloodline.11 days = 20 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					is_woman_trigger = yes
					is_alternate_start = no
					independent = yes
					lower_tier_than = KING
					NOT = { realm_size = 80 }
					NOR = { culture = myken culture = lucian }
					OR = {
						culture_group = sartosan_group
						culture = marcheur
						culture = comarcano
						culture = asur_colonial
						culture = oltremari
						culture = neueslander
					}
					capital_scope = { region = world_old_world_border_princes }
				}
			}
			ROOT = { save_event_target_as = defeated_emperor }
			FROM = {
				set_character_flag = flag_offensive_teuta
				narrative_event = { id = bloodline.11 days = 20 }
			}
		}
	}
}

narrative_event = {
	id = bloodline.11
	title = EVTTITLEbloodline11
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = flag_offensive_teuta } text = EVTDESCAbloodline11 }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = flag_defensive_teuta } text = EVTDESCBbloodline11 }
	picture = GFX_evt_norse_tempest
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_war
	sound = bloodline_added

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Holy Fury"
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAbloodline11
		if = { limit = { has_nickname = no } give_nickname = nick_the_liberator }
		if = {
			limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
			create_bloodline = { type = teuta_female }
			new_bloodline = { set_description = "bloodline_desc_teuta" }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_player = {
				limit = { is_within_diplo_range = ROOT NOT = { character = ROOT } }
				narrative_event = { id = bloodline.12 }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBbloodline11
		tiered_piety_reward_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
	after = {
		clr_character_flag = flag_defensive_teuta
		clr_character_flag = flag_offensive_teuta
	}
}

	id = bloodline.12
	title = EVTTITLEbloodline12
	desc = {
		trigger = { OR = { has_landed_title = k_luccini has_landed_title = k_myken } }
		text = EVTDESCAbloodline12
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOR = { has_landed_title = k_luccini has_landed_title = k_myken } }
		text = EVTDESCBbloodline12
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_norse_tempest
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_war
	sound = bloodline_added

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Holy Fury"
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAbloodline12
		trigger = { OR = { has_landed_title = k_luccini has_landed_title = k_myken } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBbloodline12
		trigger = { NOR = { has_landed_title = k_luccini has_landed_title = k_myken } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
}

#On_war_started: Corsair ships arrive for war.
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.13
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	war = yes

	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Holy Fury"
		OR = {
			FROM = {
				age = 16
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				any_owned_bloodline = {
					bloodline_is_active_for = PREV
					has_bloodline_flag = bloodline_spawned_ships
				}
				any_realm_province = { port = yes }
			}
			ROOT = {
				age = 16
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				any_owned_bloodline = {
					bloodline_is_active_for = PREV
					has_bloodline_flag = bloodline_spawned_ships
				}
				any_realm_province = { port = yes }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					age = 16
					NOT = { trait = incapable }
					any_owned_bloodline = {
						bloodline_is_active_for = PREV
						has_bloodline_flag = bloodline_spawned_ships
					}
					any_realm_province = { port = yes }
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				random_list = {
					25 = {
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = brave }
						modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = viking }
						modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = pirate }
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = ravager }
						modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = seaking }
						modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = sea_queen }
						character_event = { id = bloodline.14 days = 25 random = 40 }
					}
					50 = { character_event = { id = bloodline.15 days = 25 random = 40 } }
					25 = {
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = craven }
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 martial = 10 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 martial = 14 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 martial = 18 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 prestige = 500 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 prestige = 1000 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 prestige = 2000 }
						character_event = { id = bloodline.16 days = 25 random = 40 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					age = 16
					NOT = { trait = incapable }
					any_owned_bloodline = {
						has_bloodline_flag = bloodline_spawned_ships
						bloodline_is_active_for = PREV
					}
					any_realm_province = { port = yes }
				}
			}
			ROOT = {
				random_list = {
					15 = {
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = brave }
						modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = viking }
						modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = pirate }
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = ravager }
						modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = seaking }
						modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = sea_queen }
						character_event = { id = bloodline.14 days = 25 random = 40 }
					}
					60 = { character_event = { id = bloodline.15 days = 25 random = 40 } }
					25 = {
						modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = craven }
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 martial = 10 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 martial = 14 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 martial = 18 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 prestige = 500 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 prestige = 1000 }
						modifier = { factor = 0.75 prestige = 2000 }
						character_event = { id = bloodline.16 days = 25 random = 40 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

#Lots of ships arrive.
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.14
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_bloodline = {
				has_bloodline_flag = bloodline_spawned_ships
				founder = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCbloodline14_A
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			any_owned_bloodline = {
				has_bloodline_flag = bloodline_spawned_ships
				founder = { NOT = { character = ROOT } }
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCbloodline14_B
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_norse_tempest
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		any_owned_bloodline = {
			limit = { has_bloodline_flag = bloodline_spawned_ships bloodline_is_active_for = PREV }
			founder = {
				save_event_target_as = pirate_bloodline_target #Localization use only.
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAbloodline14
		spawn_fleet = {
			province = closest # closest sea zone
			owner = ROOT
			disband_on_peace = yes
			troops = { galleys = { 100 100 } }
			earmark = pirate_conscripts
		}
	}
}

##################################################################
##################################################################
# Forge Bloodline Ambition events

#Hidden checks and counters first:

#On_death: check for murders.
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.17
	hide_window = yes
	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Holy Fury"
		OR = { death_murder_unknown_trigger = yes death_murder_known_trigger = yes }
		killer = { #Not necessary to have the ambition to stack up the variable, but necessary to unlock the bloodline.
			is_playable = yes
			is_landed = yes
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = flag_about_to_murder_bloodline }
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		killer = { murder_bloodline_check_effect = yes }
	}
}

#on_war_ended_victory: check for war variable.
# From = Attacker, Root = Defender
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.18
	hide_window = yes
	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Holy Fury"
		FROM = {
			NOR = {
				trait = heresiarch
				trait = peasant_leader
				trait = adventurer
				has_character_flag = flag_about_to_war_bloodline
			}
		}
		ROOT = { NOR = { trait = heresiarch trait = peasant_leader trait = adventurer } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = {
			hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = bloodline_won_wars value = 1 } }
			if = { #Pure War Bloodline.
				limit = {
					check_variable = { which = bloodline_won_wars value = 30 }
					has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
				}
				set_character_flag = flag_about_to_war_bloodline
				character_event = { id = bloodline.31 days = 25 random = 25 }
			}
			else_if = { # Murder War Hybrid.
				limit = {
					check_variable = { which = bloodline_murdered_people value = 40 }
					check_variable = { which = bloodline_won_wars value = 20 }
					has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
				}
				set_character_flag = flag_about_to_war_bloodline
				narrative_event = { id = bloodline.45 days = 25 random = 25 }
			}
		}
	}
}

#on_war_ended_defeat: check for war variable.
# From = Attacker, Root = Defender
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.19
	hide_window = yes
	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Holy Fury"
		FROM = { NOR = { trait = heresiarch trait = peasant_leader trait = adventurer } }
		ROOT = {
			NOR = {
				trait = heresiarch
				trait = peasant_leader
				trait = adventurer
				has_character_flag = flag_about_to_war_bloodline
			}
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		ROOT = {
			hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = bloodline_won_wars value = 1 } }
			if = { #Pure War Bloodline.
				limit = {
					check_variable = { which = bloodline_won_wars value = 30 }
					has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
				}
				set_character_flag = flag_about_to_war_bloodline
				character_event = { id = bloodline.31 days = 25 random = 25 }
			}
			else_if = { # Murder War Hybrid.
				limit = {
					check_variable = { which = bloodline_murdered_people value = 40 }
					check_variable = { which = bloodline_won_wars value = 20 }
					has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
				}
				set_character_flag = flag_about_to_war_bloodline
				narrative_event = { id = bloodline.45 days = 25 random = 25 }
			}
		}
	}
}

#Fired from ambition: check years at peace.
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.20
	hide_window = yes
	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Holy Fury"
		has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
		NOT = { check_variable = { which = bloodline_peaceful_years value = 30 } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = bloodline_peaceful_years value = 1 } }
		if = { #Pure Peace Bloodline.
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = bloodline_peaceful_years value = 30 }
				has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
			}
			character_event = { id = bloodline.37 days = 25 random = 25 }
		}
		else_if = { #Murder Peace Hybrid.
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = bloodline_peaceful_years value = 15 }
				check_variable = { which = bloodline_murdered_people value = 20 }
				has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
			}
			narrative_event = { id = bloodline.46 days = 25 random = 25 }
		}
		else_if = { #Peace Architect Hybrid.
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = bloodline_peaceful_years value = 15 }
				check_variable = { which = bloodline_built_cities value = 5 }
				has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
			}
			narrative_event = { id = bloodline.49 days = 25 random = 25 }
		}
		else_if = { #Peace Castellan Hybrid.
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = bloodline_peaceful_years value = 15 }
				check_variable = { which = bloodline_built_castles value = 5 }
				has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
			}
			narrative_event = { id = bloodline.50 days = 25 random = 25 }
		}
		else_if = { #Peace Monumental Hybrid.
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = bloodline_peaceful_years value = 15 }
				check_variable = { which = bloodline_built_temples value = 5 }
				has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
			}
			narrative_event = { id = bloodline.51 days = 25 random = 25 }
		}
		else = { # Fire again, with yearly delay.
			character_event = { id = bloodline.20 days = 365 }
		}
	}
}

#on_war_started: reset peaceful_years variable.
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.21
	hide_window = yes
	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Holy Fury"
		has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { check_variable = { which = bloodline_peaceful_years value = 1 } } }
			ROOT = { set_variable = { which = bloodline_peaceful_years value = 0 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { check_variable = { which = bloodline_peaceful_years value = 1 } } }
			FROM = { set_variable = { which = bloodline_peaceful_years value = 0 } }
		}
	}
}

#on_settlement_construction_completed: check the Architect Bloodline.
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.22
	hide_window = yes
	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Holy Fury"
		FROM = { holding_type = CITY }
		NOT = { has_character_flag = flag_about_to_builder_bloodline }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		change_variable = { which = bloodline_built_cities value = 1 }
		change_variable = { which = bloodline_built_holdings value = 1 }
		if = { #First check for pure Architect Bloodline.
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = bloodline_built_cities value = 10 }
				has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
			}
			set_character_flag = flag_about_to_builder_bloodline
			narrative_event = { id = bloodline.42 days = 25 random = 25 }
		}
		else_if = { # Then check for Builder Hybrid.
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = bloodline_built_holdings value = 24 }
				has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
			}
			set_character_flag = flag_about_to_builder_bloodline
			narrative_event = { id = bloodline.47 days = 25 random = 25 }
		}
		else_if = { #Then check for Murder Builder Hybrid.
			limit = {
				killer = {
					check_variable = { which = bloodline_murdered_people value = 40 }
					check_variable = { which = bloodline_built_holdings value = 15 }
					has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = flag_about_to_builder_bloodline
			narrative_event = { id = bloodline.48 days = 25 random = 25 }
		}
		else_if = { #Peace Architect Hybrid.
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = bloodline_peaceful_years value = 15 }
				check_variable = { which = bloodline_built_cities value = 5 }
				has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
			}
			set_character_flag = flag_about_to_builder_bloodline
			narrative_event = { id = bloodline.49 days = 25 random = 25 }
		}
	}
}

#on_settlement_construction_completed: check the Castellan Bloodline.
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.23
	hide_window = yes
	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Holy Fury"
		FROM = { holding_type = CASTLE }
		NOT = { has_character_flag = flag_about_to_builder_bloodline }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		change_variable = { which = bloodline_built_castles value = 1 }
		change_variable = { which = bloodline_built_holdings value = 1 }
		if = { #First check for pure Castellan Bloodline.
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = bloodline_built_castles value = 10 }
				has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
			}
			set_character_flag = flag_about_to_builder_bloodline
			narrative_event = { id = bloodline.43 days = 25 random = 25 }
		}
		else_if = { # Then check for Builder Hybrid.
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = bloodline_built_holdings value = 24 }
				has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
			}
			set_character_flag = flag_about_to_builder_bloodline
			narrative_event = { id = bloodline.47 days = 25 random = 25 }
		}
		else_if = { #Then check for Murder Builder Hybrid.
			limit = {
				killer = {
					check_variable = { which = bloodline_murdered_people value = 40 }
					check_variable = { which = bloodline_built_holdings value = 15 }
					has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = flag_about_to_builder_bloodline
			narrative_event = { id = bloodline.48 days = 25 random = 25 }
		}
		else_if = { #Peace Castellan Hybrid.
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = bloodline_peaceful_years value = 15 }
				check_variable = { which = bloodline_built_castles value = 5 }
				has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
			}
			set_character_flag = flag_about_to_builder_bloodline
			narrative_event = { id = bloodline.50 days = 25 random = 25 }
		}
	}
}

#on_settlement_construction_completed: check the Monumental Bloodline.
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.24
	hide_window = yes
	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Holy Fury"
		FROM = { holding_type = TEMPLE }
		NOT = { has_character_flag = flag_about_to_builder_bloodline }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		change_variable = { which = bloodline_built_temples value = 1 }
		change_variable = { which = bloodline_built_holdings value = 1 }
		if = { #First check for pure Monumental Bloodline.
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = bloodline_built_temples value = 10 }
				has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
			}
			set_character_flag = flag_about_to_builder_bloodline
			narrative_event = { id = bloodline.44 days = 25 random = 25 }
		}
		else_if = { # Then check for Builder Hybrid.
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = bloodline_built_holdings value = 24 }
				has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
			}
			set_character_flag = flag_about_to_builder_bloodline
			narrative_event = { id = bloodline.47 days = 25 random = 25 }
		}
		else_if = { #Then check for Murder Builder Hybrid.
			limit = {
				killer = {
					check_variable = { which = bloodline_murdered_people value = 40 }
					check_variable = { which = bloodline_built_holdings value = 15 }
					has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = flag_about_to_builder_bloodline
			narrative_event = { id = bloodline.48 days = 25 random = 25 }
		}
		else_if = { #Peace Monumental Hybrid.
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = bloodline_peaceful_years value = 15 }
				check_variable = { which = bloodline_built_temples value = 5 }
				has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
			}
			set_character_flag = flag_about_to_builder_bloodline
			narrative_event = { id = bloodline.51 days = 25 random = 25 }
		}
	}
}

#Bloodline notification events for chosen path:

#Pure Murder Bloodline crossroad.
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.25
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Holy Fury"
		NOT = {
			any_owned_bloodline = {
				has_bloodline_flag = created_bloodline
				founder = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
		has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = { #First check if qualifies for Death little helper flavor.
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { age = 16 }
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = flag_forge_bloodline_was_child
						NOT = { age = 35 } #Should still be somewhat young.
					}
				}
			}
			narrative_event = { id = bloodline.26 }
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = bloodline.27 } #Otherwise let player decide the flavor.
		}
	}
}

#Child gains Pure Murder Bloodline.
narrative_event = {
	id = bloodline.26
	title = EVTTITLEbloodline26
	desc = { trigger = { age = 25 } text = EVTDESCbloodline26_A }
	desc = { trigger = { NOT = { age = 25 } } text = EVTDESCbloodline26_B }
	picture = GFX_evt_child_reading
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_intrigue
	sound = bloodline_added
	hide_from = yes
	show_from_from = no

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Holy Fury"
		NOT = {
			any_owned_bloodline = {
				has_bloodline_flag = created_bloodline
				founder = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
		has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
	}

	option = { #Gain Murder bloodline.
		name = EVTOPTAbloodline26
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = cruel } } add_trait = cruel }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { this_has_super_cool_nickname_trigger = no }
				random_list = {
					10 = { give_nickname = nick_the_deaths_child }
					10 = { give_nickname = nick_the_snakeling }
					10 = { give_nickname = nick_the_little_spider }
					10 = { give_nickname = nick_the_eerie }
					10 = { give_nickname = nick_the_little_nightmare }
				}
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { is_female = no } create_bloodline = { type = pure_murder_bloodline_01 } }
		if = {
			limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
			create_bloodline = { type = pure_murder_bloodline_01 inheritance = matrilineal }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
}

#Adult crossroad for pure Murder Bloodline.
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.27
	desc = EVTDESCAbloodline27
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Holy Fury"
		NOT = {
			any_owned_bloodline = {
				has_bloodline_flag = created_bloodline
				founder = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
		has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# Lunatic Dragon-King Bloodline.
	# Prop up embalmed corpses in Macabre Feast.
	# Hide everything.
	# Entrap next victim.

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTZbloodline27
		custom_tooltip = { text = TT_EVTOPTZbloodline27 }
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				trait = lunatic_1
				trait = lunatic_2
				trait = lunatic_3
				trait = possessed
				trait = cannibal_trait
			}
			trait = cruel
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = cannibal_trait } } add_trait = cannibal_trait }
		if = {
			limit = { this_has_super_cool_nickname_trigger = no is_female = no }
			ROOT = { give_nickname = nick_the_dragon }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { this_has_super_cool_nickname_trigger = no is_woman_trigger = yes }
			ROOT = { give_nickname = nick_the_dragoness }
		}
		tooltip = { #Show bloodline effects
			create_bloodline = { type = pure_murder_bloodline_05 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { narrative_event = { id = bloodline.231 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAbloodline27
		custom_tooltip = { text = TT_EVTOPTAbloodline27 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = cruel } } add_trait = cruel }
		if = {
			limit = { NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
			random = {
				chance = 5
				add_trait = lunatic_1
				set_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 5 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { this_has_super_cool_nickname_trigger = no culture_group = latin }
			ROOT = { give_nickname = nick_the_ferrante }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { this_has_super_cool_nickname_trigger = no NOT = { culture_group = latin } }
			ROOT = { give_nickname = nick_the_iron_heart }
		}
		tooltip = { #Show bloodline effects
			create_bloodline = { type = pure_murder_bloodline_02 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { narrative_event = { id = bloodline.28 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBbloodline27
		custom_tooltip = { text = TT_EVTOPTBbloodline27 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = paranoid } } add_trait = paranoid }
		if = {
			limit = { this_has_super_cool_nickname_trigger = no is_female = no }
			ROOT = { give_nickname = nick_the_pale_spider }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { this_has_super_cool_nickname_trigger = no is_woman_trigger = yes }
			ROOT = { give_nickname = nick_the_grim_mistress }
		}
		tooltip = { #Show bloodline effects
			create_bloodline = { type = pure_murder_bloodline_03 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { narrative_event = { id = bloodline.29 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCbloodline27
		custom_tooltip = { text = TT_EVTOPTCbloodline27 }
		trigger = { NOR = { is_unattractive_trigger = yes trait = celibate trait = eunuch } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = cruel } } add_trait = cruel }
		if = {
			limit = { this_has_super_cool_nickname_trigger = no is_female = no }
			ROOT = { give_nickname = nick_the_ripper }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { this_has_super_cool_nickname_trigger = no is_woman_trigger = yes }
			ROOT = { give_nickname = nick_the_black_widow }
		}
		tooltip = { #Show bloodline effects
			create_bloodline = { type = pure_murder_bloodline_04 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { narrative_event = { id = bloodline.30 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
}

			create_bloodline = { type = pure_war_bloodline_04 inheritance = matrilineal }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
}

#Pure Peace Bloodline crossroad.
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.37
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Holy Fury"
		NOT = {
			any_owned_bloodline = {
				has_bloodline_flag = created_bloodline
				founder = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
		has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = { #First check if qualifies for blessed child flavor.
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { age = 16 }
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = flag_forge_bloodline_was_child
						NOT = { age = 45 } #Should still be somewhat young.
					}
				}
			}
			narrative_event = { id = bloodline.38 }
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = bloodline.39 } #Otherwise let player decide the flavor.
		}
	}
}

#Child gains Pure Peace Bloodline.
narrative_event = {
	id = bloodline.38
	title = EVTTITLEbloodline38
	desc = EVTDESCbloodline38
	picture = GFX_evt_child_talking
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_diplomacy
	sound = bloodline_added

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Holy Fury"
		NOT = {
			any_owned_bloodline = {
				has_bloodline_flag = created_bloodline
				founder = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
		has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
	}

	option = { #Gain Peace bloodline.
		name = EVTOPTAbloodline38
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = kind } } add_trait = kind }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { this_has_super_cool_nickname_trigger = no }
				random_list = {
					10 = { trigger = { religion = grail } give_nickname = nick_the_angel }
					10 = { give_nickname = nick_the_pure }
					10 = { give_nickname = nick_the_celestial }
					10 = { give_nickname = nick_the_radiant }
					10 = { give_nickname = nick_the_innocent }
				}
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { is_female = no } create_bloodline = { type = pure_peace_bloodline_01 } }
		if = {
			limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
			create_bloodline = { type = pure_peace_bloodline_01 inheritance = matrilineal }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
}

	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

		NOT = {
			any_owned_bloodline = {
				has_bloodline_flag = created_bloodline
				founder = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
		has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
	}

	# Protect realm.
	# Issue great celebrations everywhere.

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAbloodline39
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = kind } } add_trait = kind }
		if = {
			limit = { this_has_super_cool_nickname_trigger = no }
			ROOT = { give_nickname = nick_the_peace_maker }
		}
		tooltip = { #Show bloodline effects
			create_bloodline = { type = pure_peace_bloodline_02 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { narrative_event = { id = bloodline.40 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBbloodline39
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = charitable } } add_trait = charitable }
		if = {
			limit = { this_has_super_cool_nickname_trigger = no }
			ROOT = { give_nickname = nick_the_munificent }
		}
		tooltip = { #Show bloodline effects
			create_bloodline = { type = pure_peace_bloodline_03 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { narrative_event = { id = bloodline.41 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}
}

	desc = EVTDESCbloodline52

		trigger = { has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal }
		picture = GFX_evt_matriarchy_throne_room
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_religion_feature = religion_patriarchal }
		picture = GFX_evt_tengri_throneroom_oldgods
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				has_religion_feature = religion_patriarchal
				has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
			}
		}
		picture = GFX_evt_bloodlines
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Holy Fury"
		NOT = {
			any_owned_bloodline = {
				has_bloodline_flag = created_bloodline
				founder = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
		has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
	}

	option = { #Becoming Exemplar ruler (Benevolent/Family)
		name = EVTOPTAbloodline52
		custom_tooltip = { text = TT_EVTOPTAbloodline52 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = bloodline.53 days = 150 random = 150 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
	option = { #Improving the realm (Religious I/Technology)
		name = EVTOPTBbloodline52
		custom_tooltip = { text = TT_EVTOPTBbloodline52 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = bloodline.54 days = 150 random = 150 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
	option = { #Becoming Feared ruler (Religious II/Ruthless)
		name = EVTOPTCbloodline52
		custom_tooltip = { text = TT_EVTOPTCbloodline52 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = bloodline.55 days = 150 random = 150 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
	option = { #Refuse event chain.
		name = EVTOPTDbloodline52
		custom_tooltip = { text = TT_EVTOPTDbloodline52 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
}

#Gaining Benevolent Bloodline.
#First Row. - Revising old realm laws to improve conditions of vassals and peasants.
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.56
	title = EVTTITLEbloodline56
	desc = EVTDESCbloodline56
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal }
		picture = GFX_evt_matriarchy_throne_room
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_religion_feature = religion_patriarchal }
		picture = GFX_evt_tengri_throneroom_oldgods
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				has_religion_feature = religion_patriarchal
				has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
			}
		}
		picture = GFX_evt_bloodlines
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Holy Fury"
		NOT = {
			any_owned_bloodline = {
				has_bloodline_flag = created_bloodline
				founder = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
		has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
	}

	#Special learning option. - Revise laws yourself.
	#Hire scholars to look at and revise old unfair laws.
	#Try revising old laws.
	#Dedicate more time to court hearings.
	#Abandon quest for Bloodline.

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAbloodline56
		trigger = { learning = 18 }
		tooltip_info = learning
		custom_tooltip = { text = TT_EVTOPTAbloodline56 }
		if = { limit = { trait = arbitrary } remove_trait = arbitrary }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = bloodline.57 days = 150 random = 250 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBbloodline56
		custom_tooltip = { text = TT_EVTOPTBbloodline56 }
		if = { limit = { trait = arbitrary } remove_trait = arbitrary }
		scaled_wealth = { value = -2 min = -500 max = -900 }
		prestige = -2000
		add_character_modifier = { name = forging_legend_revisiting_laws_1 years = 5 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = bloodline.57 days = 500 random = 500 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCbloodline56
		custom_tooltip = { text = TT_EVTOPTCbloodline56 }
		if = { limit = { trait = arbitrary } remove_trait = arbitrary }
		prestige = -1500
		add_character_modifier = { name = forging_legend_revisiting_laws_2 years = 5 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = bloodline.57 days = 1000 random = 1000 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDbloodline56
		trigger = { NOT = { learning = 18 } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = TT_EVTOPTDbloodline56 }
		if = { limit = { trait = arbitrary } remove_trait = arbitrary }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = stressed } } add_trait = stressed }
		else_if = { limit = { trait = stressed } health = -0.5 }
		prestige = -900
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = bloodline.57 days = 2000 random = 2000 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTEbloodline56
		custom_tooltip = { text = TT_EVTOPTEbloodline56 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = content } } random = { chance = 25 add_trait = content } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

#Benevolent Bloodline gained.
narrative_event = {
	id = bloodline.58
	title = EVTTITLEbloodline56
	desc = EVTDESCbloodline58
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal }
		picture = GFX_evt_matriarchy_throne_room
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = { has_religion_feature = religion_patriarchal }
		picture = GFX_evt_tengri_throneroom_oldgods
	}
	picture = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				has_religion_feature = religion_patriarchal
				has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
			}
		}
		picture = GFX_evt_bloodlines
	}
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_diplomacy
	sound = bloodline_added

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_dlc = "Holy Fury"
		NOT = {
			any_owned_bloodline = {
				has_bloodline_flag = created_bloodline
				founder = { character = ROOT }
			}
		}
		has_ambition = obj_forge_bloodline
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAbloodline58
		if = {
			limit = {
				this_has_cool_nickname_trigger = no
				this_has_super_cool_nickname_trigger = no
			}
			ROOT = { give_nickname = nick_the_benevolent }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_female = no }
			create_bloodline = { type = event_benevolent_bloodline_01 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
			create_bloodline = { type = event_benevolent_bloodline_01 inheritance = matrilineal }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
}

				this_has_cool_nickname_trigger = no
				this_has_super_cool_nickname_trigger = no
			}
			ROOT = { give_nickname = nick_the_theologian }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { religion_group = old_world_group }
			if = {
				limit = { is_female = no }
				create_bloodline = { type = event_religion_peaceful_bloodline_01 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				create_bloodline = {
					type = event_religion_peaceful_bloodline_01
					inheritance = matrilineal
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group }
			if = {
				limit = { is_female = no }
				create_bloodline = { type = event_religion_peaceful_bloodline_02 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				create_bloodline = {
					type = event_religion_peaceful_bloodline_02
					inheritance = matrilineal
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {

			if = {
				limit = { is_female = no }
				create_bloodline = { type = event_religion_peaceful_bloodline_03 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				create_bloodline = {
					type = event_religion_peaceful_bloodline_03
					inheritance = matrilineal
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { religion_group = chaos_gods_group }
			if = {
				limit = { is_female = no }
				create_bloodline = { type = event_religion_peaceful_bloodline_04 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				create_bloodline = {
					type = event_religion_peaceful_bloodline_04
					inheritance = matrilineal
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { religion_group = cadai_group }
			if = {
				limit = { is_female = no }
				create_bloodline = { type = event_religion_peaceful_bloodline_05 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes }
				create_bloodline = {

					inheritance = matrilineal

		name = EVTOPTCbloodline72
		custom_tooltip = { text = TT_EVTOPTCbloodline56 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = cruel } } add_trait = cruel }
		scaled_wealth = { value = -2 min = -350 max = -600 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = bloodline.73 days = 1000 random = 1000 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDbloodline72
		trigger = { NOT = { intrigue = 18 } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = TT_EVTOPTDbloodline56 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = cruel } } add_trait = cruel }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = stressed } } add_trait = stressed }
		else_if = { limit = { trait = stressed } health = -0.5 }
		prestige = -600
		add_character_modifier = { name = forging_legend_martial_law_2 years = 5 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = bloodline.73 days = 2000 random = 2000 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTEbloodline72
		custom_tooltip = { text = TT_EVTOPTEbloodline56 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = content } } random = { chance = 25 add_trait = content } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

	}
}

#Murder Child
#On_childhood pulse: Ward is bored.
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.100
	desc = EVTDESCAbloodline100
	picture = GFX_evt_child_reading
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	min_age = 12
	max_age = 16

	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		any_owned_bloodline = {
			has_bloodline_flag = bloodline_murder_child
			bloodline_is_active_for = PREV
		}
		intrigue = 3
		NOR = {
			trait = incapable
			trait = maimed
			trait = mangled
			trait = kind
			trait = idolizer
			trait = affectionate
		}
		OR = { trait = cruel trait = deceitful trait = brooding trait = haughty }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				educator = { is_alive = yes prisoner = no }
				educator = {
					NOR = {
						is_close_relative = ROOT
						trait = incapable
						is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
						reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 50 }
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				guardian = { is_alive = yes prisoner = no }
				guardian = {
					NOR = {
						is_close_relative = ROOT
						trait = incapable
						is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
						reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 50 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				guardian = { is_alive = yes prisoner = no }
				guardian = {
					NOR = {
						is_close_relative = ROOT
						trait = incapable
						is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
						reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 50 }
					}
				}
			}
			guardian = { save_event_target_as = scoped_guardian }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				educator = { is_alive = yes prisoner = no }
				educator = {
					NOR = {
						is_close_relative = ROOT
						trait = incapable
						is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
						reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 50 }
					}
				}
			}
			educator = { save_event_target_as = scoped_guardian }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAbloodline100
		custom_tooltip = { text = TT_EVTOPTAbloodline100 }
		random = { chance = 50 change_intrigue = 1 }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:scoped_guardian = { character_event = { id = bloodline.101 } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBbloodline100
		custom_tooltip = { text = TT_EVTOPTBbloodline100 }
	}
}

#Murder deflection
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.107
	desc = EVTDESCbloodline107
	picture = GFX_evt_meeting_hf
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAbloodline107
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				trait = kinslayer
				trait = familial_kinslayer
				trait = dynastic_kinslayer
				trait = tribal_kinslayer
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { intrigue = 15 }
			if = { limit = { trait = kinslayer } prestige = -500 remove_trait = kinslayer }
			if = {
				limit = { trait = familial_kinslayer }
				prestige = -250
				remove_trait = familial_kinslayer
			}
			if = {
				limit = { trait = dynastic_kinslayer }
				prestige = -150
				remove_trait = dynastic_kinslayer
			}
			if = {
				limit = { trait = tribal_kinslayer }
				prestige = -150
				remove_trait = tribal_kinslayer
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = { limit = { trait = kinslayer } prestige = -1000 remove_trait = kinslayer }
			if = {
				limit = { trait = familial_kinslayer }
				prestige = -500
				remove_trait = familial_kinslayer
			}
			if = {
				limit = { trait = dynastic_kinslayer }
				prestige = -300
				remove_trait = dynastic_kinslayer
			}
			if = {
				limit = { trait = tribal_kinslayer }
				prestige = -300
				remove_trait = tribal_kinslayer
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBbloodline107
		trigger = { has_character_modifier = known_murderer }
		if = {
			limit = { intrigue = 15 }
			prestige = -500
			remove_character_modifier = known_murderer
		}
		else = { prestige = -750 remove_character_modifier = known_murderer }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCbloodline107
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_character_modifier = vicious_rumors
				has_character_modifier = vicious_rumors_2
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { intrigue = 15 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = vicious_rumors_2 }
				prestige = -250
				remove_character_modifier = vicious_rumors_2
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = vicious_rumors }
				prestige = -150
				remove_character_modifier = vicious_rumors
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = vicious_rumors_2 }
				prestige = -500
				remove_character_modifier = vicious_rumors_2
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = vicious_rumors }
				prestige = -300
				remove_character_modifier = vicious_rumors
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDbloodline107
		hidden_tooltip = { remove_character_modifier = deflection_decision_cooldown }
		set_character_flag = bloodline_skip_after_effects # Changed my mind: no after-effects.
	}
	after = { #After-effects
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = bloodline_skip_after_effects } }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				random_list = {
					#Stress
					#Paranoia
					#Deceit
					#Cruelty
					#Lunacy
					250 = { }
					10 = {
						trigger = { NOT = { trait = stressed } }
						character_event = { id = bloodline.108 days = 25 random = 25 }
					}
					10 = {
						trigger = { NOT = { trait = paranoid } }
						character_event = { id = bloodline.109 days = 25 random = 25 }
					}
					10 = {
						trigger = { NOT = { trait = deceitful } }
						character_event = { id = bloodline.110 days = 25 random = 25 }
					}
					10 = {
						trigger = { NOT = { trait = cruel } }
						character_event = { id = bloodline.111 days = 25 random = 25 }
					}
					1 = {
						trigger = {
							NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
						}
						character_event = { id = bloodline.112 days = 25 random = 25 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = bloodline_skip_after_effects
	}
}

#Plotter sees an opportunity - assassinate
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.113
	desc = EVTDESCbloodline113
	picture = { trigger = { ROOT = { is_woman_trigger = no } } picture = GFX_evt_cornered_duel_hf }
	picture = { trigger = { ROOT = { is_woman_trigger = yes } } picture = GFX_evt_slit_throat_hf }
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_part_of_plot = yes
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		any_owned_bloodline = {
			has_bloodline_flag = bloodline_murder_stalk
			bloodline_is_active_for = PREV
		}
		OR = { has_plot = plot_kill_character has_plot = plot_kill_spouse }
		plot_target_char = {
			OR = { at_location = ROOT ROOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = no } }
			age = 16
			NOR = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes trait = incapable prisoner = yes }
		}
		NOT = { has_character_flag = murder_in_motion }
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 3000

		modifier = { factor = 0.9 plot_power = 0.1 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.9 plot_power = 0.2 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.9 plot_power = 0.3 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 plot_power = 0.4 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 plot_power = 0.5 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 plot_power = 0.6 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 plot_power = 0.7 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 plot_power = 0.8 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power = 0.9 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 plot_power = 1.0 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.6 plot_power = 1.5 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 plot_power = 2.0 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.9 intrigue = 10 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.9 intrigue = 12 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.9 intrigue = 14 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 intrigue = 16 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.7 intrigue = 18 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.6 intrigue = 20 }
	}

	immediate = {
		plot_target_char = { save_event_target_as = plot_target }
		save_event_target_as = assassin_plot_owner
	}

	#Do it
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAbloodline113
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTA_bloodline113_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:plot_target = { character_event = { id = bloodline.114 days = 7 } }
			set_character_flag = murder_in_motion
		}
	}

	#Wait
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBbloodline113
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTB_bloodline113_TOOLTIP }
	}
}

#Assassin notified of "Plot target dies, undiscovered"
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.116
	desc = EVTDESCbloodline116
	picture = { trigger = { ROOT = { is_woman_trigger = no } } picture = GFX_evt_cornered_duel_hf }
	picture = { trigger = { ROOT = { is_woman_trigger = yes } } picture = GFX_evt_slit_throat_hf }
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAbloodline116
		FROM = {
			if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes }
			else = {
				FROM = {
					death = {
						death_reason = death_murder_unknown
						killer = event_target:assassin_plot_owner
					}
				}
			}
		}
		#If quest holder, clear quest and reward
		if = {
			limit = {
				society_member_of = the_assassins
				has_quest = quest_the_assassins_assassination
				quest_target = { character = event_target:plot_target }
			}
			clr_quest = quest_the_assassins_assassination
			sound_effect = assassins_assassinate_character

			if = {
				limit = { event_target:plot_target = { higher_tier_than = DUKE } }
				add_society_currency_major_effect = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:plot_target = { tier = DUKE } }
				add_society_currency_medium_effect = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:plot_target = { lower_tier_than = DUKE } }
				add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = murder_in_motion
	}
}

# Knight Bloodline
# Bards/Knights arriving at court.
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.129
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_playable = yes
	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no
	min_age = 16

	trigger = {
		piety = 50
		any_owned_bloodline = {
			has_bloodline_flag = bloodline_knights
			bloodline_is_active_for = PREV
			founder = { NOT = { character = ROOT } religion_group = old_world_group }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			60 = { #Knight
				character_event = { id = bloodline.130 }
			}
			60 = { #Bard
				character_event = { id = bloodline.131 }
			}
			80 = {
				modifier = { factor = 3 ai = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.35 NOT = { higher_tier_than = COUNT } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.35 NOT = { higher_tier_than = DUKE } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.75 is_evil_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 0.65 is_benevolent_trigger = yes }
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.35
					any_courtier_or_vassal = { count = 2 has_bloodline_flag = bloodline_knights }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.35
					any_courtier_or_vassal = { count = 3 has_bloodline_flag = bloodline_knights }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

character_event = { # Knight character arrives
	id = bloodline.130
	desc = EVTDESCbloodline130
	picture = GFX_evt_western_knights_byzantine
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_playable = yes
	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no
	min_age = 16

	immediate = {
		spawn_fantastic_commander_effect = yes
		event_target:invited_character = {
			set_character_flag = bloodline_great_hero
			set_character_flag = hero_knight
			remove_trait = cruel
			remove_trait = craven
			add_trait = kind
			add_trait = cavalry_leader
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAbloodline130

		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { ROOT = { has_law = succ_byzantine_elective } } #Either not Imperial...
				NOT = { #...Or there are no valid vassal candidates that can be commanders.
					any_vassal = {
						higher_real_tier_than = BARON
						NOT = { primary_title = { temporary = yes } }
						OR = {
							AND = {
								OR = {
									male_can_hold_minor_title_trigger = yes
									female_can_hold_minor_commander_title_trigger = yes
								}
								is_adult = yes
							}
							has_character_flag = special_marshal
						}

						prisoner = no
						NOT = { trait = incapable }
						NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
						NOT = { has_character_flag = guru }
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = refusing_to_lead }
						NOR = {
							has_job_title = job_chancellor
							has_job_title = job_marshal
							has_job_title = job_treasurer
							has_job_title = job_spymaster
							has_job_title = job_spiritual
							has_minor_title = title_commander
						}
					}
				}
			}
			any_realm_character = {
				NOT = { higher_tier_than = BARON }
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_commander
				NOT = { martial = 12 }
			}
			OR = {
				AND = {
					tier = COUNT
					any_realm_character = {
						count = 2
						liege = { character = ROOT }
						has_minor_title = title_commander
					}
				}
				AND = {
					tier = DUKE
					any_realm_character = {
						count = 4
						liege = { character = ROOT }
						has_minor_title = title_commander
					}
				}
				AND = {
					tier = KING
					is_nomadic = no
					any_realm_character = {
						count = 6
						liege = { character = ROOT }
						has_minor_title = title_commander
					}
				}
				AND = {
					tier = EMPEROR
					is_nomadic = no
					any_realm_character = {
						count = 8
						liege = { character = ROOT }
						has_minor_title = title_commander
					}
				}
				AND = {
					tier = KING
					is_nomadic = yes
					any_realm_character = {
						count = 2
						liege = { character = ROOT }
						has_minor_title = title_commander
					}
				}
				AND = {
					tier = EMPEROR
					is_nomadic = yes
					any_realm_character = {
						count = 4
						liege = { character = ROOT }
						has_minor_title = title_commander
					}
				}
			}
		}

		random_realm_character = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { higher_tier_than = BARON }
				liege = { character = ROOT }
				has_minor_title = title_commander
				NOT = { martial = 12 }
			}
			remove_title = title_commander
			opinion = { modifier = insulted who = ROOT years = 2 }
		}

		event_target:invited_character = {
			give_minor_title = title_commander
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_loyal_servant who = ROOT years = 100 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAbloodline130

		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { ROOT = { has_law = succ_byzantine_elective } } #Either not Imperial...
				NOT = { #...Or there are no valid vassal candidates that can be commanders.
					any_vassal = {
						higher_real_tier_than = BARON
						NOT = { primary_title = { temporary = yes } }
						OR = {
							AND = {
								OR = {
									male_can_hold_minor_title_trigger = yes
									female_can_hold_minor_commander_title_trigger = yes
								}
								is_adult = yes
							}
							has_character_flag = special_marshal
						}

						prisoner = no
						NOT = { trait = incapable }
						NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
						NOT = { has_character_flag = guru }
						NOT = { has_character_modifier = refusing_to_lead }
						NOR = {

							has_job_title = job_marshal
							has_job_title = job_treasurer
							has_job_title = job_spymaster
							has_job_title = job_spiritual
							has_minor_title = title_commander

					}

#Landless ladies of same religion might ask for protection.
character_event = {
	id = bloodline.132
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_women = yes
	religion_group = old_world_group
	min_age = 16
	max_age = 30
	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		is_ruler = no
		is_woman_trigger = yes
		NOT = { has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal }
		is_married = no
		NOT = { num_of_children = 1 }
		NOT = { num_of_lovers = 1 }
		NOT = { num_of_friends = 1 }
		NOR = { trait = nun trait = brave trait = content }
		NOT = { has_character_flag = special_marshal }
		liege = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { religion = ROOT }
				NOT = { reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = -40 } }
				is_rival = ROOT
			}
			any_neighbor_independent_ruler = {
				age = 16
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				any_owned_bloodline = {
					has_bloodline_flag = bloodline_knights
					bloodline_is_active_for = PREV
				}
				religion = ROOT
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random = {
			chance = 5
			random_neighbor_independent_ruler = {
				limit = {
					any_owned_bloodline = {
						has_bloodline_flag = bloodline_knights
						bloodline_is_active_for = PREV
					}
					religion = ROOT
				}
				character_event = { id = bloodline.133 }
			}
		}
	}
}

character_event = { # Woman asks for assistance
	id = bloodline.133
	desc = EVTDESCbloodline133
	picture = GFX_evt_homage_hf
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Marry her.
	#Host her.
	#Refuse her.

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAbloodline133
		trigger = {
			is_benevolent_trigger = yes
			is_married = no
			is_female = no
			FROM = { is_married = no }
		}
		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			liege = {
				show_scope_change = no
				opinion = { modifier = opinion_outraged who = ROOT years = 100 }
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_saved_me who = ROOT years = 100 }
			move_character = ROOT
			if = { limit = { is_betrothed = yes } break_betrothal = yes }
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_married = yes #Multiplayer delay
				}
				remove_spouse = yes
			}
			add_spouse = ROOT
			if = { limit = { is_no_females_race = no ROOT = { is_no_females_race = no } } add_lover = ROOT }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBbloodline133
		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			liege = {
				show_scope_change = no
				opinion = { modifier = opinion_outraged who = ROOT years = 100 }
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_saved_me who = ROOT years = 100 }
			move_character = ROOT
			if = { limit = { is_betrothed = yes } break_betrothal = yes }
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_married = yes #Multiplayer delay
				}
				remove_spouse = yes
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCbloodline133
		tiered_piety_negative_effect = yes
		FROM = { opinion = { modifier = opinion_dishonorable_scum who = ROOT years = 100 } }
	}
}

	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAbloodline134
		if = { limit = { OR = { trait = strategist martial = 15 } } prestige = -300 }
		if = { limit = { NOR = { trait = strategist martial = 15 } } prestige = -600 }
		add_character_modifier = { name = infiltration_tactics years = 4 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 25 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBbloodline134
		if = { limit = { OR = { trait = strategist martial = 15 } } prestige = -300 }
		if = { limit = { NOR = { trait = strategist martial = 15 } } prestige = -600 }
		add_character_modifier = { name = polished_formation years = 4 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 25 }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCbloodline134
		if = { limit = { OR = { trait = strategist martial = 15 } } prestige = -200 }
		if = { limit = { NOR = { trait = strategist martial = 15 } } prestige = -400 }
		add_character_modifier = { name = advanced_supply_lines years = 4 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 75 }
	}
	option = { #Changed my mind.
		name = EVTOPTDbloodline134
		hidden_tooltip = { remove_character_modifier = drill_troops_cooldown }
		set_character_flag = bloodline_skip_after_effects # Changed my mind: no after-effects.
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
	after = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = bloodline_skip_after_effects } }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				random_list = {
					90 = { }
					3 = {
						trigger = { NOR = { trait = uncouth trait = robust } }
						modifier = { factor = 2 trait = brave }
						modifier = { factor = 2 trait = wroth }
						character_event = { id = bloodline.135 days = 20 random = 20 }
					}
					3 = {
						trigger = { NOT = { trait = wroth } }
						modifier = { factor = 2 trait = aggressive_leader }
						modifier = { factor = 2 trait = brave }
						modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cruel }
						character_event = { id = bloodline.136 days = 20 random = 20 }
					}
					3 = {
						trigger = { NOT = { trait = is_malnourished } }
						modifier = { factor = 2 trait = temperate }
						modifier = { factor = 2 trait = stressed }
						character_event = { id = bloodline.137 days = 20 random = 20 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = bloodline_skip_after_effects
	}
}

		name = EVTOPTBbloodline135
		if = { limit = { trait = robust } change_martial = 1 }
		else_if = { limit = { NOR = { trait = feeble trait = robust } } add_trait = robust }
		else_if = { limit = { trait = feeble } remove_trait = feeble }
		if = { limit = { trait = uncouth } change_diplomacy = -1 }
		else_if = { limit = { NOR = { trait = uncouth trait = groomed } } add_trait = uncouth }
		else_if = { limit = { trait = groomed } remove_trait = groomed }
		ai_chance = { factor = 75 }
	}
}
